  its underlying ground changes, with its former state remaining in the Record
- Human timing governing Withdrawal; the player protecting, relocating, or
  adapting what matters rather than defeating the work itself
- A rare personal MEET able to bring the Founding Escape back through the body
  of one surviving Founder, as detailed in Appendix F

Road Work follows the shared event arc below. Its Impact is the onset of active
work; its Persistence may include several advances and a final human
Withdrawal before the altered ground enters Aftermath.

### Events Through Time

Environmental events unfold through a persistent four-phase arc.

| Phase | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **Telegraph** | The world shows signs of what may happen while preparation can still alter the stakes. |
| **Impact** | The pressure arrives and acts upon the preparation, Readiness, bodies, and Places already present. |
| **Persistence** | The condition continues, changes, or produces a situation requiring attention. |
| **Aftermath** | Damage, displacement, care, repair, changed routes, memory, and other consequences remain. |

- Not every phase opening MEET; a warning able to remain ambient until the
  player has a meaningful choice
- Persistence carrying an unresolved situation beyond its first MEET when the
  world has not yet changed enough to settle it
- Impact not being disguised as a leisurely decision after the event has
  already struck
- Heavy wind potentially announcing itself through bending reeds and humming
  lines before tearing an exposed covering; the storm passing while closure,
  care, repair, and memory remain
- An event not warning, striking, and repairing itself within one card; its
  consequences entering the persistent world and becoming part of its history

# PART III — HOME: COLONY, DWELL, AND EMBODY

## 3.1 Home

**Colony DWELL is MEDIAN's base-builder mode of play.** The player builds,
tends, organizes, prepares, understands, and inhabits one permanent Colony.

More precisely, **Home** is the Mode, **Colony** is its principal Register, and
**DWELL** is the operator through which the player acts upon collective life.
Together, Colony DWELL names MEDIAN's base-building experience.

### The inward Mode

- Home as the Mode in which the player's primary responsibility is the founding
  Colony
- Its direction being Inward: toward one permanent settlement, its civic
  condition, and the named lives accumulated there
- Colony and Embodiment belonging intrinsically to Home, with MEET able to bring
  a consequential Home situation into focus
- Home not being a camera, map, interface, or generic safe zone

### Four related terms

| Term | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **Home** | The Inward Mode of responsibility. |
| **Colony** | The permanent civic settlement and mechanical subject of Home play. |
| **Home Median** | The physical median occupied by the Colony. |
| **Sanctuary** | The materially, civically, and socially credible condition the Colony creates and restores. |

### One permanent center

- The founding Colony remaining the campaign's permanent center
- Growth improving, densifying, inhabiting, damaging, repairing, and remembering
  it rather than replacing it
- Outposts, Stopovers, and allied settlements never becoming
  duplicate Homes
- Corridor growth increasing the Colony's reach without moving its emotional or
  mechanical center

### Sanctuary made credible

- Sanctuary being achieved and maintained through understandable material,
  civic, and social conditions
- Sanctuary remaining relative rather than perfect: weather, scarcity, injury,
  displacement, predators, and human disturbance can still reach Home
- The player establishing and periodically restoring the conditions for
  ordinary life rather than preventing perpetual collapse
- Consequence able to enter Home without turning it into another exposure zone

### Ordinary life as the purpose

- Rest, care, work, conversation, play, ritual, relationship, and familiar
  routine as part of the civilization being built
- Stability as a successful condition rather than failed pacing
- A sufficiently sustained Colony releasing attention toward observation,
  attachment, chosen Projects, and EMBODY
- Ordinary life not being idle production Capacity

### Return changes Home

- Away mattering because particular Citizens leave a credible sanctuary
- Homecoming reintegrating material, bodily, relational, civic, and remembered
  consequences
- The Colony receiving what returned before the game becomes fully Home again
- Every return able to alter the Home from which the next departure begins

### From Home to DWELL

- Chapter 3.2 explaining how the player works upon this permanent civic subject
  through DWELL
- Later chapters explaining its Places, species placement, Roles, Practices,
  situations, resources, population, and embodied life

## 3.2 DWELL

DWELL is the ongoing stewardship of the Colony in Home. It remains open by
default: the player chooses where to look, what responsibility to sustain, and
which lasting changes justify committed effort.

### The Colony in view

The Colony is DWELL's mechanical subject, but every Citizen remains named and
visible within its collective accounting. The player reads and arranges Home
through Places, Practices, Roles, Projects, resources, and situated conditions.
An elevated, freely navigable view makes material, civic, and social state
legible without reducing DWELL to its camera.

### Attention and time

Routine information and continuing work remain native to DWELL rather than
seizing attention. Looking, reading, planning, and moving the camera consume no
time; a meaningful Home commitment advances the shared clock by one Day Band,
and DAWN resolves the completed day while surfacing only meaningful change.
Launch and Homecoming connect DWELL to Away through MEET.

### Civic Balance: Shares, Load, Readiness, and Pressure

**Civic Balance** describes whether the Colony can carry the weight created by
its own growth. Housing and each Role remain separate responsibilities, so
strength in one domain never conceals a shortfall in another. Citizens direct
their Civic Shares either to ordinary Role coverage or to Projects. Practices
add situated physical support, but neither perform labor nor advance Projects
without Citizens.

#### Shared balance grammar

Each axis compares Capacity with its full Load. Surplus becomes **Readiness**,
an exact match is **Covered**, and uncovered Load becomes **Pressure**. An axis
with no present obligation is N/A rather than an empty post the player is
expected to fill. Pressure therefore names a specific vulnerability, not a
single Colony-wide deficit.

#### Role Balance

**Civic Share** is Body-Unit-normalized ordinary civic contribution: Rabbits,
Squirrels, and v0.5 Guests contribute one; Mice contribute one-half. It measures
aggregate responsibility rather than aptitude, authority, or personal worth.
Growth adds Role Load through more lives, dependencies, inhabited ground,
Practices, routes, and Stores. Only present, available Citizens sustaining that
Role answer its Load.

#### Housing Balance

Core Citizens create Housing Load by Body Unit. A normal Residence supports two
Body Units; a Well-Placed one supports three. Thus the same Residence houses
two Rabbits or Squirrels—or four Mice—and good placement raises those numbers
to three or six. Lost capacity creates Housing Pressure but never evicts a
Citizen. Guest housing remains separate and follows each species' Residence
Fit.

#### Readiness and Pressure

Role Readiness is the Civic Share remaining after that Role's present Load is
covered; Role Pressure is whatever remains uncovered. Both stay attached to
their Role. Readiness supports stability and better response without granting
immunity from events or consequence.

#### Capacity and materials

Civic Capacity answers ordinary responsibility without continuously consuming
Stores. Resources instead enable Projects, preparation, recovery, and protected
stakes during MEET; they cannot purchase exemption from Load or replace
sustaining Citizens.

#### Civic Pressure, World Pressure, and Colony Pressure

The separate Housing and Role Pressures form a **Civic Pressure profile**.
**World Pressure** supplies external conditions. A hidden, momentary **Colony
Pressure** calculation weighs both with current circumstances, persistence,
bounded randomness, and recent Home MEETs to decide when vulnerability becomes
a situation. The strongest relevant shortfall normally supplies its primary
civic stake; another shortfall or the actual Citizen, Place, relationship,
resource, or environmental circumstance supplies the collateral stake.
Pressure creates vulnerability rather than automatic daily damage.

#### Practice Strength

A completed usable Practice has situated **Practice Strength** when a situation
meaningfully involves it. Its committed physical order contributes even when
Role support is thin; compatible Civic Shares strengthen it, and good Species
placement adds the important Spatial Alignment bonus. Support aggregates
automatically, with no Citizen-to-Practice linking. Practice Strength helps
shape a situated response but never erases standing Role Load.

#### Civic choice and Projects

Each available Citizen directs their Civic Share either to ordinary Role work
or to an active Project. Commitment advances lasting change while withdrawing
that share from Role coverage, Practice support, and any passive throughput.
Several Citizens may divide a Role's effort between continuity and change, but
infrastructure alone never advances a Project.

#### Passive production and transformation

Passive output exists only where a Role and Practice define one, and requires
both a usable relevant Practice and an actual Citizen sustaining the production
Role. Practices, sustaining Civic Shares, and good placement add to throughput;
fractions accumulate until DAWN releases whole chunks. Projects, inputs,
reserves, recipes, and season then create meaningful tradeoffs. The relationship
never invents output for a Practice that defines none.

### Quiet and consequential attention

A well-sustained Colony remains in DWELL without compulsory intervention.
Pressure stays ordinary information until stakes, choice, and consequence bring
a bounded situation into MEET. **Quiet Equilibrium**, Home's sole tracked
condition, becomes active while ordinary obligations are covered and no acute
situation demands attention. It opens EMBODY without becoming a resource or
reward; observation and voluntary Projects remain available regardless.

## 3.3 Places and Species Placement

A **Place** is physical ground designated within Home. A Builder designates that
ground at zero cost. A vacant designation may be withdrawn freely; committing
its first Residence or Practice Project establishes the Place permanently. An
established Place may stand vacant or hold one Residence or Practice. Only an
explicit Guest exception departs from that rule. Species placement determines
how effectively the established use serves the Colony.

### Place uses

An established Place retains its location, footprint, physical condition,
species relationship, history, and use. A Practice gives the Place its
functional name—Garden, Hearth, Workshop—without a separate building type. The
same Practice keeps the same civic purpose across Core Species even as its form
changes. Places may become vacant, damaged, adapted, or transformed, but
lasting consequences remain attached to that ground. Development always makes
a specific new fact true rather than leveling the Place automatically.

### Builder designates Places

Designation costs no material, effort, or time and produces no civic benefit by
itself. Establishing a Residence or Practice requires a Builder Project; its
commitment makes the Place permanent, and its unfinished use supplies no
Capacity, strength, or output. Completion makes that use available. The first
Workshop is the sole bootstrap exception. Builder makes the physical fact but
does not own the completed Practice or later Role-specific adaptation.

### Core Residence

A Residence is an inhabited use of Place, not a Practice. Its Body-Unit
Capacity answers the Housing Load of accepted Citizens without granting or
withholding belonging. Shortfall creates Housing Pressure and never eviction.
Core Residences share the capacity relationship introduced in DWELL; Guest
residences occupy dedicated Places and follow the Residence Fit of their
species unless that species defines a specific Practice-sharing exception.
Residences supply no Practice Strength or passive output.

### Species placement

Every usable Residence and Practice is **Well Placed** or **Not Well Placed**
according to the Colony's spatial operator. Mouse **JOIN** values contact,
enclosure, and adjacency; Rabbit **GATHER** values open common space, mutual
visibility, and protective edges; Squirrel **CONNECT** values reachable nodes,
elevation, alternate routes, and redundancy. This judgment comes from spatial
relationships rather than a species-themed skin. Guest placement follows the
housing needs of the individual Guest species.

### Spatial Alignment

A Well-Placed Residence gains fifty percent more Housing Capacity. A
Well-Placed Practice gains a substantial situated-strength bonus and, where it
defines passive production, additional throughput. Alignment never replaces
the required Practice-and-Citizen gate. Because it follows the completed
layout, later growth can improve or degrade a Place—for example, by obscuring a
Rabbit court's common center. The interface previews these effects before
commitment. Placement thereby makes Species spatial identity mechanically
important without creating three parallel civic systems.

### Place development

Sustained use, season, civic need, consequence, a Guest, or the Rest-Stop
Metropolis may reveal a specific improvement. The possibility becomes directly
available rather than entering a Knowledge track; MEET presents it when a
meaningful choice is needed, and the appropriate Role's Project makes it real.
An improvement changes one Place and adds a specific capability, response,
range, or resilience rather than a generic level. A Practice gains an
improvement only where a real capability warrants it; the system does not
manufacture improvements to make the Practice list symmetric. Winter
Cultivation, for example, adapts one Garden for limited winter production
without changing every Garden.

## 3.4 Roles and Practices

The Civic Balance established in Chapter 3.2 governs every Role and Practice
below. Sustaining Civic Shares cover the Load of their Roles. Completed
Practices provide situated Practice Strength according to compatible Role
support and placement. Project commitment withdraws Citizens and their Civic
Shares from ordinary responsibility, Practice support, and passive output.

### Roles and their Practices

MEDIAN has six standing Roles. Each is first established through its civic
responsibility, ordinary contribution, related Practices, characteristic
Projects, vulnerabilities, and limits. Shared Practices appear under every
Role that uses them and are identified together after the Role entries so that
none appears to belong to the first Role that uses it.

| Role | Practices |
|---|---|
| **Builder** | Workshop |
| **Gardener** | Garden |
| **Crafter** | Workshop |
| **Caretaker** | Hearth; Storehouse |
| **Watchkeeper** | Watchpost |
| **Leader** | Gathering Place |

### Builder

Builder carries responsibility for the physical soundness and deliberate
transformation of Home. **Workshop** is Builder's principal Practice: land,
material, access, and established methods committed to making and maintaining
the Colony's physical fabric. Other Practices may need Builder work without
becoming Builder Practices themselves.

#### The first Workshop

The Colony may establish its first Workshop without already having one. This
sole bootstrap exception still requires material, time, and committed Builder
Citizens; the unfinished Workshop supplies no Practice Strength, and its
completion ends the exception. Every later Workshop follows the ordinary
Project rules.

#### Work and vulnerability

Ordinary Builder work keeps coverings, supports, drainage, access, passages,
and connections sound. When that responsibility is covered, Home needs no
repair order for every wall or ladder. Builder Projects instead make a lasting
physical fact true: establishing a Residence or Practice, reinforcing a route
or shelter, restoring major damage, constructing an Outpost, or completing
major physical or Tier-defining work. Committed Builders leave ordinary
Readiness, so growth temporarily trades some structural assurance for lasting
change.

A Builder shortfall makes Home vulnerable to wind, water, damaged access,
failed anchors, softened ground, and structural overextension. MEET locates
that vulnerability in actual inhabited ground and normally resolves the
immediate consequence; a restoration Project follows only when physical work
genuinely remains. Tool-making belongs to Crafter. Guest Signatures may reveal
a hidden circumstance or improve a response, but they do not replace Builder
responsibility or Citizen labor.

### Gardener

Gardener carries responsibility for cultivation, seasonal yield, and the
continuing health of cultivated ground. **Garden** is Gardener's Practice: land,
soil, access, and accumulated order committed to growth. Builder establishes a
Garden through an ordinary Project after the first Workshop; an unfinished
Garden supplies neither Practice Strength nor Sustenance.

#### Passive production

Perishable Sustenance follows the shared additive production rule: usable
Gardens, sustaining Gardener Civic Shares, and Well-Placed Gardens each add to
yield. Both a usable Garden and an actual sustaining Gardener are required.
Committing Gardeners to Projects therefore reduces ordinary production as well
as Gardener Readiness. Season and situation modify the resulting yield rather
than bypassing that gate.

#### Winter Cultivation

An ordinary Garden produces no regular winter Sustenance. Learned **Winter
Cultivation** becomes real only when a Gardener Project physically adapts one
Garden. With Gardener coverage, that Garden then produces at one-quarter of
normal yield and uses the same fractional accumulation as other passive
throughput.

#### Work and vulnerability

Ordinary Gardener work tends growth, soil, protection, and continuity between
yields. Projects instead establish or reshape cultivated ground, restore
persistent damage, or make a lasting seasonal or ecological change. Builder
may supply the physical work without taking the Garden's continuing purpose
from Gardener.

A Gardener shortfall makes Home vulnerable to drought, heat, washout, crop
loss, damaged soil, poor yield, and winter shortage. MEET locates consequence
in an actual Garden, family, reserve, or threatened part of Home. Guest
Signatures may change those circumstances or open a lasting Project, but they
do not replace Gardener coverage or the production gate.

### Crafter

Crafter transforms recovered material into functional civic and Away
capability. **Workshop** supports both Crafter and Builder without becoming two
Practices or requiring Citizens to be assigned to individual Workshops. Its
protected ground, organized material, access, and established methods support
work; Citizens still perform it.

#### Supply Preparation

**Supply Preparation** passively converts available inputs into Binding,
Device, and Offering Supplies through the shared Practice-and-Citizen rule.
Recipes, Stores, targets, and reserves bound the result; Caretaker
Provisioning prepares Remedies separately. These prepared Supplies remain
anonymous expedition resources rather than named possessions. A Crafter
committed to a Project leaves both ordinary Readiness and Supply Preparation.

#### Tools and Crafter Projects

Tools are deliberate, durable Away-function Items rather than passive output.
Making one requires a Crafter Project, appropriate Scrap, time, a usable
Workshop, and committed Crafters. Ordinary maintenance remains covered work;
adaptation becomes a Project when it makes a new persistent fact true. Crafter
Projects may also establish a lasting fabrication capability or defined civic
artifact. Collaboration never changes which Role owns the intended result.

#### Fine Work

**Fine Work** is a Workshop improvement that opens Specialist Tools. Each has a
specific authored capability beyond the five ordinary Tool classes, its own
learned recipe, and its own Crafter Project. Specialist Tools are reproducible,
individually held one-slot Tools rather than singular Special Artifacts. Fine
Work increases neither ordinary throughput nor Project capacity.

A Crafter shortfall makes Supplies unavailable, Tools or substitutions fail,
or material become disordered when a particular need arises. MEET situates the
consequence rather than imposing routine breakage or fabrication chores. Tools
remain made for work rather than violence. Guest Signatures may reveal a
material possibility or response, but they do not bypass recipes, Projects, or
Crafter responsibility.

### Caretaker

Caretaker carries responsibility for receiving, provisioning, nurture,
dependency, medical readiness, recovery, and the preservation of ordinary
life. Its Civic Shares sustain one civic responsibility across **Hearth** and
**Storehouse** without assignment to individual Practices.

#### Hearth: care of lives

**Hearth** is the foundational shared Practice of nurture, dependency, and
recovery—not a family unit. Caretakers use it to feed, settle, clean, and
comfort vulnerable Citizens; diagnose and stabilize injury; manage illness;
receive injured or exhausted returnees; care for young Citizens and Patients;
guide recovery; and help displaced or newly arrived lives enter ordinary life.
Young Citizens' bodily dependency remains Caretaker work at Hearth. Their later
learning and integration create Leader Load through Gathering Place and
ordinary Colony life rather than a second Hearth responsibility.

Named Patients, incapacity, treatment, and recovery remain individually
legible while Load and Readiness remain Colony-scale. A Colony without a
Patient or relevant health circumstance carries no automatic medical Load.
A Wounded Citizen at Home adds temporary Patient Load equal to that Citizen's
Body Unit. The obligation enters Caretaker Load once and remains there until
the Wound clears; when Capacity does not cover it, the difference is ordinary
Caretaker Pressure rather than a separate injury penalty. The Patient's own
unavailability remains individually true throughout recovery.

#### Storehouse: care of Stores

The Colony's **Stores** form one pooled resource state. A **Storehouse** is the
later Caretaker Practice that gives those resources a physical presence in
DWELL and supports their receiving, ordering, protection, and transformation.
Selecting any Storehouse opens the same globally available Resources screen;
multiple Storehouses never divide Stores into local inventories.

Storehouse supports one shared **Caretaker Provisioning** process. Its
throughput is allocated between preserving Perishable Sustenance as Durable
Sustenance and preparing Remedy Supplies from defined inputs. Known methods,
targets, protected reserves, and available inputs bound both outputs. The same
Caretaker Civic Share cannot supply full Preservation and full Remedy
Preparation simultaneously. A Project-committed Caretaker leaves ordinary
care and reduces this shared throughput.

Before the first Storehouse is completed, the Founders' carried Stores remain
in one visible provisional cache and in the globally accessible Resources
screen. The cache provides no Provisioning throughput or Practice Strength.
Establishing the first Storehouse uses the ordinary Project Queue after the
first Workshop; it receives no second bootstrap exception.

If one of several Storehouses becomes unusable, its Practice Strength and
throughput cease. Pooled Stores remain available through the Resources screen
unless an explicit event spoils, destroys, isolates, or otherwise changes the
resources themselves.

#### Receiving, recovery, and Projects

Receiving remains Caretaker work rather than a separate Practice. Homecoming,
hospitality, displacement, and aftermath may bring Citizens, Patients, Guests,
Wanderers, food, Scrap, Supplies, or threatened reserves into Hearth,
Storehouse, Residence, or MEET as the situation requires.

Ordinary Caretaker work receives, diagnoses, stabilizes, treats, preserves,
prepares, orders, and guides recovery. Projects make a persistent result true:
adapting Hearth or Storehouse, restoring displaced or contaminated care,
establishing a lasting preservation or treatment capability, completing
individualized Rehabilitation, resettling displaced lives, or making durable
seasonal preparation. Immediate treatment, Wound recovery, routine receiving
and movement of Stores, ordinary Provisioning, and target changes remain
ordinary work.

#### Wound Recovery and Rehabilitation

Wound recovery is ordinary Caretaker work rather than a Project. It enters no
Project Queue, reserves no material, and commits no named Caretaker. One
Wounded Citizen occupies one usable Hearth for an authored requirement of one
to three **Recovery Days**. That Hearth must be free of another Wounded Citizen,
an active Young-care commitment, and any other intensive use that already
occupies it. While recovery occupies the Hearth, it cannot support another
Wounded Citizen, Young-care commitment, or Hearth Project, though it retains
ordinary Practice Strength. If several Citizens could claim limited Hearths, a
Care MEET makes the allocation; when only one valid allocation exists, it
occurs without an empty choice.

Each Home DAWN records one Recovery Day for a Home-present Wounded Citizen
occupying a Hearth. No fractional Recovery Day accumulates. Additional
Hearths or Caretakers cannot accelerate the biological interval. Caretaker
Pressure may create situated vulnerability or a Care MEET, but it does not by
itself stop an occupied Hearth's recovery clock. Without an available Hearth,
the Wound and its Patient Load persist and no Recovery Day advances.
Completion clears the Wound and its Patient Load and releases the Hearth while
leaving any Maiming and its history true. Remedy stabilization neither removes
nor shortens this Home requirement.

**Rehabilitation** improves one Hearth for one individualized Project at a
time. Protected movement space, bodily supports, and sustained care may help a
Maimed Citizen establish an adapted way to move, work, communicate, or
eventually Launch. It does not promise restoration, erase Maiming or history,
or accelerate Wound recovery. A separate civic or cultural consequence may
create Leader work, but it never becomes Hearth support or part of the
Rehabilitation gate. Wound recovery requires an available Hearth but not this
improvement.

A Caretaker shortfall makes Home vulnerable to spoilage, disordered or
inaccessible Stores, crowding, displacement, failed receiving, unmet
dependency, family disruption, delayed recovery, untreated injury or illness,
exhausted care, inadequate Remedies, contamination, clustered Patient need,
and poor winter preparation. MEET or Homecoming locates consequence in the
actual Citizen, Storehouse, Hearth, family, or reserve rather than inflicting
anonymous daily harm. Guest Signatures may reveal a condition, contain
aftermath, or open a response without replacing Caretaker responsibility.

### Watchkeeper

Watchkeeper carries the singular civic responsibility of noticing pressure
early enough for deliberate response. It governs warning and Telegraph rather
than generalized defense, and any number of available Citizens may sustain it.

#### Watchpost

**Watchpost** is Watchkeeper's sole Practice. It preserves sightlines and
listening lines, known approaches, warning markers, protected observation, and
communication into inhabited Home. Edges, routes, and high or listening points
are circumstances of Watchpost rather than additional Practices. Basic watch
is possible before one exists, so Watchpost needs no bootstrap exception.

#### Standing responsibility

Ordinary watch attends Home's approaches, water, human activity, predators,
route obstruction, weather, and unusual sound, vibration, scent, or absence.
Adequate Readiness produces earlier, clearer, better-localized Telegraph and
better circumstances for response. It supplies useful time and information,
not exact probabilities or prevention. Project-committed Watchkeepers leave
ordinary warning, while temporary observation or forecast work remains a civic
commitment unless it creates a persistent result.

#### Watchkeeper Projects

Watchkeeper Projects adapt Watchpost, extend warning to new inhabited ground,
establish or restore a lasting signal, or answer a recurring pressure. Builder
or Crafter may support the physical means without taking the Project's warning
purpose from Watchkeeper. Once warning is adequate, the Role has no compulsory
repeatable Project.

#### Far Warning

**Far Warning** improves one Watchpost to identify the family of an eligible
approaching pressure early enough for another preparation window. It remains
qualitative: no exact probability, countdown, location, severity, or guarantee
that every pressure can be seen.

A Watchkeeper shortfall makes warning late, incomplete, or badly localized,
narrowing the Colony's response before pressure reaches inhabited ground.
MEET locates that consequence in the Place, route, Residence, Practice, or
Citizen at risk. Shortfall never permits catastrophic loss to occur unseen
while the player is Away. Watchkeeper is neither guard nor combat class;
Resident Signatures may create distinctive warnings or responses without
replacing ordinary watch.

### Leader

Leader carries responsibility for the Colony's shared meaning and shared
action: coordination, deliberation, agreement, hospitality, integration,
practical and cultural transmission, social memory, observance, ceremony, and
the civic recognition of major transitions. **Gathering Place** is Leader's
sole Practice. Leader supplies no Hearth support.

#### Gathering Place

Gathering Place is recognized common ground where Citizens interpret shared
life and make consequential choices together. Lessons, Laws and Sayings,
storytelling, newcomer integration, memorials, Maturity, arrivals, After-names,
Tier recognition, collective deliberation, ceremony, and dispute resolution
may occur there through MEET or ordinary life.

#### Community Board

**Community Board** improves one Gathering Place by giving selected Almanac
information already known to the Colony persistent shared expression in DWELL.
Its physical board may stand elsewhere in Home while remaining part of that
Practice. It neither discovers nor improves information and gates no civic
decision; the Almanac remains available without it.

#### Open Table

**Open Table** improves one Gathering Place for rare large-cast Presence. A
Leader Project establishes protected common use, several simultaneous points
of attention, and safe access suited to the Colony's actual bodies and spatial
form; no literal human table is required. Completion makes an Open Table
invitation eligible but never launches one automatically.

An invitation still requires Home, Quiet Equilibrium, a safe usable improved
Gathering Place, a present eligible gathering, and an authored occasion. No
particular Citizen or active Leader is required after completion. Several Open
Table improvements never stack or create a capacity score; the actual
gathering owns the invitation. A shared meal may be present without becoming a
resource cost or the definition of the experience.

#### Standing responsibility

Leader Load arises from actual coordination, dispute, hospitality, agreement,
emergency, transition, practical or cultural transmission, newcomer
integration, memorial duty, young-Citizen learning, or another shared
commitment. Without such a need, an unstaffed Leader Role creates no Pressure.
Adequate Readiness helps the Colony understand, choose, and act without
guaranteeing agreement, removing sacrifice, or replacing the Role that owns a
material problem. Leadership remains responsibility among equals, not rank,
command, official doctrine, or universal social competence.

#### Leader Projects

Leader Projects establish a lasting civic agreement, signal, communication
function, Gathering Place adaptation, Community Board, memorial, observance,
integrated consequential discovery, or Tier-defining change. Ordinary lessons,
storytelling, welcome, interpretation, deliberation, dispute handling,
information display, Maturity, and Tier-recognition ceremony remain civic
transactions rather than Projects.

A Leader shortfall makes Home vulnerable to delayed decisions, unresolved
disagreement, uneven sacrifice, failed coordination, social strain, failed
newcomer integration, interrupted practical or cultural transmission,
neglected grief or memorial duty, lost shared meaning, and unmet learning needs
among young Citizens. MEET locates that consequence in a real Citizen,
decision, relationship, Gathering Place, memorial, transition, or shared
pressure without deleting known facts, halting Maturity automatically,
removing player agency, or making one Citizen sovereign. Leader is not wildcard
Role support and produces no passive output, research progression, culture
currency, or Maturity-speed bonus. Guest knowledge may enter communal life
through leadership without changing Leader contribution.

### Shared Practices

**Workshop** is shared by Builder and Crafter. **Hearth** and **Storehouse** are
Caretaker Practices; **Gathering Place** is Leader's Practice. A Practice may
host several kinds of ordinary life without acquiring another compatible Role.
Hearth sustains treatment, nurture, bodily dependency, and recovery through
Caretaker Readiness. Learning, integration, and shared meaning belong to Leader
through Gathering Place and ordinary Colony life. **Family** remains a domestic
relationship, separate from Hearth. Storehouse is Caretaker's resource-facing
Practice rather than a shared inventory divided among Places.

### Project Queue

The **Project Queue** is the shared execution layer for deliberate work that
makes a defined, persistent result true.

#### Project capacity

Each completed usable Practice accommodates one active Project, so the Colony's
physical development creates its capacity for deliberate change. The
supporting Practice and target may be different Places: a Workshop can support
a Builder establishing a Garden elsewhere, for example. Multiple suitable
Practices permit concurrent Projects, but no Practice advances one without
committed Citizens. Builder's first-Workshop exception is the sole bootstrap.

#### Creating an entry

The player chooses a persistent result, its supporting Practice, material, and
named Citizens of the Role that owns the result. Establishing a Residence,
Practice, or Outpost belongs to Builder; creating a Tool belongs to Crafter;
Winter Cultivation belongs to Gardener; Rehabilitation belongs to Caretaker.

#### Queue operations

Before commitment, the interface shows the resulting loss of Role coverage,
Practice support, and passive throughput; reserved material can still be
released. Commitment occupies the Practice and withdraws the named Citizens
from ordinary work. Their Civic Share-Days accumulate until DAWN records whole
progress. A committed Project cannot be cancelled or interrupted. Completion
makes the result true, releases the Practice, and returns its Citizens to
ordinary availability. The Practice retains whatever situated strength its
remaining compatible support provides while the Project proceeds.

Wound recovery never enters the Project Queue. Its Patient Load, Hearth
occupancy, and Recovery Days follow the ordinary-care rule above while Projects
remain reserved for persistent changes such as Rehabilitation.

## 3.5 Pressure, Situations, and Home MEETs

Pressure shows where the Colony may struggle when weather, scarcity, danger,
disagreement, or another real circumstance tests it. Consequence arises through
the concrete situation and its resolution.

If the Colony can absorb that test through Readiness, ordinary life continues
and the player receives a brief account of what happened. If the test presents
meaningful stakes, choice, and consequence, DWELL yields to a Home MEET.

### World Pressure

**World Pressure** is an externally originating corridor condition that may add
contextual Load, test Readiness, alter ordinary life, or supply the circumstance
for a Home situation. Its origin lies outside civic allocation even though the
Colony can prepare for and mediate its consequences.

Its families include atmosphere; water and ground; biological life; human
maintenance; and traffic or machinery. Biome, Season, Day Band, and special
events change their intensity, duration, forecastability, and footprint rather
than creating separate engines. Home adjudicates them only at meaningful state
changes. Severe Pressure can open MEET even in a Covered Colony, while a civic
shortfall can become consequential under otherwise ordinary conditions.

Road Work is the defined exception to ordinary absorption. Once active, it
supplies the Colony's primary MEET thread regardless of Civic coverage. The
Colony's Readiness and situated strengths change what the player can protect
and what each response costs; they do not reduce active human work to a routine
DWELL report. Ordinary Pressure situations wait while the episode advances,
while Homecoming and urgent independent situations may still require their own
MEETs. Active Road Work is an acute situation, so Quiet Equilibrium and EMBODY
resume only after the work has withdrawn and its immediate demands have been
settled.

### Bringing Pressure into focus

World conditions establish plausible situation families; the Civic Pressure
profile shows where they may find purchase. Hidden Colony Pressure recalculates
at meaningful checks, using bounded randomness and campaign history so timing
remains uncertain but causal. Recent deficiencies and situation families are
temporarily damped to prevent mechanical repetition, while persistent problems
may return later with deeper stakes. The leading shortfall supplies the primary
stake; another weakness or concrete circumstance supplies the collateral one.
Readiness and the involved Practice then help decide whether the test remains
an ordinary report or enters MEET.

### Telegraph and rebalancing

Watchkeeping is the general source of **Telegraph**; Guest Signatures may add
bounded species-specific warning. Telegraph identifies an observable family,
urgency, exposed Roles, or relevant Places without exact probabilities. When it
arrives early enough, the player may reassign uncommitted Civic Shares, adjust
Provisioning, prepare, or knowingly accept the risk. This can change the coming
situation without guaranteeing control. The Almanac holds all known Telegraph;
Community Board may mirror it inside DWELL but creates none.

### Choosing through Home MEET

A Pressure-driven Home MEET ordinarily asks the player to protect the primary
stake, protect the collateral stake instead, or endure the situation and accept
their combined consequence. Immediate cost, protected value, and likely result
remain legible before commitment. This three-response shape belongs to Pressure
situations rather than every Home conversation or ceremony, and resolving it
consumes world time.

### Consequence and aftermath

Home danger remains part of ordinary civic life rather than becoming a defense
mode. Roles, Readiness, Practices, warning, resources, and Signatures shape the
response; consequence attaches to named Citizens and persistent parts of the
world. Injury, displacement, grief, death, or lasting change occurs only when
the situation and choice support it, and catastrophic loss never happens unseen
because attention is Away. Resolution completes the immediate situation, but
underlying Pressure remains until an actual state change restores Balance. A
Project follows only when a persistent result genuinely remains to be made.

## 3.6 Resources, Production, and Provisioning

### Resources

| Resource | Function |
|---|---|
| **Perishable Sustenance** | Green-season food; normally consumed first and vulnerable to spoilage. |
| **Durable Sustenance** | Preserved food for winter, disruption, and reserves. |
| **Flexible Scrap** | Material suited to binding, wrapping, weaving, lashing, and sealing. |
| **Rigid Scrap** | Material suited to bracing, shielding, surfacing, and reinforcement. |
| **Supplies** | Prepared, expendable capability used during Away or situated need. |
| **Tools** | Durable functional Items created deliberately through Crafter Projects. |

Resources remain pooled Colony-scale **Stores**. Scrap does not divide into
component inventories, Storehouses do not create local stockpiles, and prepared
Supplies do not retain individual makers.

### Production and transformation

Garden and Gardener produce Perishable Sustenance; Workshop and Crafter prepare
Binding, Device, and Offering Supplies; Storehouse and Caretaker supply one
shared Provisioning throughput allocated between Perishable-to-Durable
preservation and Remedy Preparation. Each process follows its defined
Practice-and-Citizen gate, gains from good placement, and remains bounded by
inputs, season, recipes, targets, and reserves. Other Practices produce nothing
merely because they exist.

### Provisioning

The player sets each eligible transformation to **Prepare** or **Hold**;
**Unavailable** explains a missing Practice, Citizen, input, method, or
condition. Targets prevent automatic overproduction, reserves protect Stores
from unrelated use, and uncommitted reservations remain visible and releasable.
Caretaker Provisioning divides one available throughput between Preservation
and Remedy Preparation rather than counting Storehouse or Caretaker support
twice. Provisioning only transforms resources that exist.

### Beautification

Beautification is the shared passive civic expression of Builder, Watchkeeper,
and Leader effort.

Their ordinary Civic Shares fill one Colony-wide track at DAWN; Project-bound
Citizens do not contribute. A threshold creates a small authored **Frill**
suited to the Role, Species, Place, and recent history. Frills appear during
Quiet Equilibrium when necessary, persist thereafter, and may invite EMBODY.
They are mechanically inert, allowing non-producing civic work to leave a
visible trace without becoming another optimization economy.

### DAWN accounting

DAWN resolves the day just completed, then establishes the state of the new day.
Results completed at that DAWN first contribute during the new day.

It consumes Sustenance, resolves active transformations and spoilage, releases
whole passive yields, advances Beautification and Projects, resolves recovery,
then recalculates Places and Civic Balance before reporting meaningful change.
Fractional contributions remain accumulated behind the scenes. Completed
Residences, Practices, and recoveries become available in the new day; newly
usable production first yields at the following DAWN.

Daily Sustenance demand is **one Sustenance per total resident Body Unit**.
Citizens Away remain resident and continue to count, so departure cannot reduce
the Colony's obligation. Body Units are totaled before demand is calculated:
two Mice therefore create the same daily demand as one Rabbit or Squirrel.

### Resources and Projects

Projects draw only their defined material and may reserve it before commitment.
Commitment protects those Stores. Builder establishes Practices; Crafter creates
Tools; lasting restoration or adaptation follows its owning Role. Ordinary
upkeep remains covered responsibility, so time alone causes no structural
decay.

## 3.7 Population and Settlement Growth

MEDIAN is a low-population colony builder. Growth adds named lives, and every
new Citizen brings both civic possibility and new responsibility. A mature
Colony remains a community of dozens whose absences, arrivals, needs, and
contributions stay legible.

### Population at Home

The **Colony Roster** includes every living named Citizen who belongs, including
young Citizens, Guests, Patients, and those Away. **Citizens at Home** identifies
who is present; **Available Civic Population** identifies who can sustain Roles
or Projects; **Dependents and Patients** identifies present lives requiring
support. Keeping these views separate prevents total population from appearing
as interchangeable labor. Absence or incapacity removes a current Civic Share,
never citizenship.

### Capability and obligation

Each available adult adds their Body-Unit Civic Share, while every new Citizen
adds needs for Sustenance, accommodation, access, care, protection,
integration, and remembrance. Young or unavailable Citizens add responsibility
before they add or recover a Civic Share. Body Units normalize civic and
material scale without making any Citizen fractional in personhood or
belonging.

### Supporting a larger Colony

A larger Colony depends on Residences, Sustenance, routes, care, Readiness, and
seasonal preparation rather than one population cap. Before planned growth,
the player sees its effects on Housing, Sustenance, Role Load, care, and Civic
Shares. Emergency refuge may exceed ordinary capacity through visible Pressure
and provisional shelter, while permanent welcome remains a deeper commitment.
Growth stays optional even when support exists.

### Three paths of growth

**Wanderers** join through refuge and integration. **Nesting** adds a few named
young Citizens through a rare seasonal commitment. **Guest residency** grows
from relationship and hospitality. Each path changes the Colony's history and
obligations rather than acting as an add-population command, and usually
resolves through Home or Homecoming even when contact began Away.

### Wanderers and arrival

Wanderers are the primary early source of adult growth. Each arrives named,
with a Prior-life Tale and any relevant injury, relationship, knowledge, or
unfinished circumstance. MEET lets the Colony offer permanent welcome,
temporary refuge, help onward, or refusal according to its real capacity.
Provisional shelter makes urgent compassion possible while exposing its Load.
An integrated Wanderer becomes an equal Core Citizen; refusal enters history
without becoming automatic punishment.

### Nesting

Nesting is a rare, voluntary, green-season Colony commitment rather than
population production. During Quiet Equilibrium, particular mature Citizens
may voice a wish to parent through an authored **Nesting Proposal** shaped by
their relationships, Tales, ages, and circumstances. The player never assigns
parents. A Home MEET asks whether the Colony can promise future accommodation,
protected Sustenance, Hearth, Caretaker support, Leader support, and protection,
and shows the
Pressure that commitment may create. The player may accept, defer, or refuse
without commanding reproduction or receiving an automatic relationship
penalty.

Success adds a very small number of fully named young Citizens. They create
care and material obligations immediately but contribute no Civic Share until
Maturity. **Young** remains one mechanical life stage but contains two
sequential civic-support spans. During the **Dependency span**, each new Body
Unit creates an enlarged Sustenance requirement, Caretaker Load, and an
intensive Hearth commitment. During the later **Learning span**, Sustenance
settles to the ordinary requirement, the Caretaker development Load and Hearth
commitment end, and Leader Load begins as the young Citizen enters practical,
cultural, and shared civic life. DAWN advances the current span; coverage
determines Pressure and situated consequence rather than accelerating or
halting the interval automatically. Exact Sustenance addition and span lengths
belong to tuning. Completion opens Maturity, ends development Load, and begins
the Citizen's ordinary Body-Unit Civic Share. Adoption and chosen family arise
through their own authored lives, outside the seasonal Nesting procedure.

### Hospitality and Guest residency

Hospitality requires the credible ability to house, sustain, know, and
integrate a Guest. Residency completes only when the species' Residence Fit and
agreed Terms of Hospitality become true. The welcomed Guest then enters the
Roster, adds ordinary obligations, and contributes one Civic Share while
available. Guest identity, Residence exceptions, Signatures, and the full path
to residency belong to Chapter 4.3; accepting residency remains optional at
every Tier.

### Scale and continuity

Every increase produces named Citizens with Tales and visible relationships to
Home. Mouse Colonies may become denser without making individual Mice partial
lives. Population bands guide encounter, visual, accommodation, and campaign
tuning without becoming a hard ceiling. A small stable Colony can be complete;
later loss changes present capability without erasing its history or achieved
Tier.

## 3.8 EMBODY

**Colony DWELL is MEDIAN's version of base-building play. EMBODY is MEDIAN's
version of cozy play.**

EMBODY lets the player live as a Citizen or remain present with Citizens inside
the sanctuary created through DWELL. Participation gives the player bounded
direct involvement through one Citizen. Presence gives the player guided,
animal-scale attention without taking control of a Citizen. Most experiences
remain intimate and small-cast. Open Table exceptionally widens Presence to a
village-scale gathering while preserving every Citizen as an individual.

### Access and invitation

- Quiet Equilibrium opening EMBODY and its loss closing EMBODY
- EMBODY remaining strictly Home-only and never becoming available at an Away
  camp, Stopover, uncontested Node, or other merely calm location
- Ordinary EMBODY experiences entering through a present and available Citizen
  at a safe, usable Place
- Open Table entering through an eligible gathering rather than through one
  Citizen
- Individual experiences appearing as invitations rather than compulsory tasks
- An unavailable Citizen or unsafe Place remaining temporarily ineligible
- A Citizen in active Tharn remaining unavailable for EMBODY
- The player being free to leave an experience without penalty

Open Table additionally requires a safe usable Gathering Place carrying the
**Open Table** improvement and a present authored gathering. The improvement
makes invitations eligible rather than automatic; no particular Citizen or
active Leader becomes an entry requirement.

### Participation and Presence

EMBODY has two primary forms:

- **Participation:** limited direct involvement in a bounded activity such as
  running, gathering, carrying, arranging, grooming, or playful movement
- **Presence:** guided observation through looking, listening, movement,
  attention, or repose, ordinarily remaining with one Citizen and a small cast
- An experience able to move naturally from Participation into Presence
- Controls remaining specific to the moment rather than expanding into
  unrestricted free movement
- Every experience carrying an authored arrival, sensory or relational
  development, and natural release
- The Citizen retaining agency over exact posture, pace, response, and social
  behavior
- Participation experiences expressing work already resolved through DWELL
  rather than producing superior output
- Cosmetic arrangements or meaningful remembered details able to persist when
  appropriate

**Open Table** is the exceptional large-cast expression of Presence. It enters
through the gathering itself: no Citizen becomes the player's acting body, and
attention may move among several simultaneous groups and activities.

### Experience families

- **Flow Traversal:** safe movement, rhythm, balance, momentum, and confidence
- **Small Work:** ordinary work felt through small tactile actions
- **Sensory Repose:** warmth, stillness, listening, breathing, and relief
- **Social Play:** chasing, teasing, imitation, curiosity, and mutual delight
- **Comfort and Care:** safety expressed through closeness, grooming, food, rest,
  and tending
- **Observation and Watch:** familiar attention to Home without threat-scanning
  play
- **Weather Enjoyment:** conditions that create Exposure Away becoming pleasure
  under shelter

These families may overlap. Open Table is a scale and presentation of Presence
rather than another experience family: a village-festival gathering may hold
Social Play, Comfort and Care, Observation, food, races, conversation,
observance, and quiet simultaneously. A shared meal may help gather the cast
without defining the form. The families organize authored experiences without
becoming separate progression tracks.

### Species embodiment

- EMBODY translating the Colony's spatial grammar into bodily experience
- Mouse **JOIN** becoming fitted adjacency, shared walls, accumulated interior,
  warmth, and nearby life
- Rabbit **GATHER** becoming open common space, mutual visibility, bounding
  movement, familiar neighbors, and protected edges
- Squirrel **CONNECT** becoming height, balance, sway, junctions, alternate
  routes, and confidence across separation
- The same Place geometry mattering in both DWELL and EMBODY
- Species expression arising through bodies, movement, scale, sound, and spatial
  relationships rather than cosmetic styling alone

### Citizens and relationships

- Most experiences belonging to a particular Citizen in a particular Place and
  remaining small-cast
- Open Table forming the bounded large-cast exception, with every visible
  participant remaining a named Citizen with their own relationships, timing,
  attention, and response rather than becoming a crowd unit
- Other Citizens retaining their own timing and responses rather than behaving
  as commanded props
- Play, affection, comfort, imitation, and attention feeling offered by
  relationships
- Injury, recovery, age, memory, and prior events shaping eligible
  experiences without reducing Citizens to condition displays
- Care experiences expressing recovery and trust without replacing Caretaker
  or Leader responsibility
- Young Citizens participating only in experiences appropriate to their present
  life stage

### Memory and reward

- The experience itself being the primary reward
- EMBODY granting no production multiplier, Civic Share, progression
  currency, or superior Citizen statistic
- Exposure ebbing through sanctuary and ordinary Home recovery, with EMBODY
  able to portray that change but never accelerate it
- EMBODY never clearing Tharn or substituting for the care and recovery that
  resolves it
- A moment entering the Record only when something particular and worth
  remembering occurs
- Repeated experiences remaining available for pleasure without manufacturing
  duplicate historical importance
- Quiet Equilibrium creating permission for attention rather than converting
  calm into a spendable resource

### Boundaries

- EMBODY remaining guided and bounded rather than becoming a second traversal
  game
- EMBODY having no hard failure, with mistakes creating texture, hesitation,
  rearrangement, or a gentler finish rather than punishment or repeated retry
- Flow Traversal emphasizing pleasure and confidence rather than lethal falls,
  harsh retries, or score pressure
- Small Work never becoming the required manual method for ordinary production
- Presence remaining meaningful even when the player's only actions are looking,
  listening, settling, approaching, or waiting
- Open Table remaining guided, bounded, and animal-scale while allowing the
  player's attention to move among simultaneous groups without inhabiting any
  participant
- EMBODY never erasing injury, grief, or other consequence merely by
  depicting comfort
- Loss of Quiet Equilibrium closing an active experience safely rather than
  nesting an urgent Home MEET inside EMBODY
- The system preserving the distinction between maintaining sanctuary through
  DWELL and inhabiting it through EMBODY

# PART IV — THE CITIZENS

Across Core and Guest species, balance means equivalent authorial completeness,
mechanical credibility, expressive richness, vulnerability, and opportunity
for attachment. Difference in body, mechanics, frequency, and magnitude is the
substance of that balance.

## 4.1 Citizenhood

MEDIAN is about a civilization small enough for every member to remain a
person. A **Citizen** is a named member of the Colony with a body, relationships,
belongings, history, present circumstances, and a possible future. Location,
age, injury, and availability may change while citizenship endures.

Citizens share the functions usually concentrated in a single player
character. Each carries a particular life, some of the Colony's capabilities,
and a distinct exposure to consequence; together, the Roster carries the
player's continuity through the world. They are fractional player characters
without becoming fractions of people.

The player should ideally come to feel attachment to—and something like
friendship with—particular Citizens. Appearance, voice, temperament, habits,
relationships, possessions, routines, and accumulated history create that
particularity without becoming personality scores or productivity ranks.

### One civic architecture

Core and Guest Citizens hold equal civic and historical standing through one
shared architecture:

| Aspect | Body-facing rule |
|---|---|
| **Belonging** | The living Roster records who belongs to the Colony. |
| **Location** | A Citizen is at Home or at one current Away position. |
| **Availability** | Age, injury, treatment, absence, or another real condition determines what they can presently contribute. |
| **Civic contribution** | An available adult supplies the Civic Share defined by their Body Unit. |
| **Continuity** | Names, relationships, Items, harm, memory, and death follow the particular Citizen across every Register. |

One Rabbit, one Squirrel, or one v0.5 Guest constitutes one Body Unit; two Mice
constitute one Body Unit while remaining two Citizens. Body Units normalize
Housing, Sustenance, Civic Balance, Projects, party scale, hazard, and Carry.
Names, relationships, personal Items, harm, memory, and death remain attached
to individual lives. This gives Mouse its intentional advantage in the number
of Citizens—and therefore personal Item positions—within the same aggregate
scale.

Young Citizens, Patients, absent travelers, and other dependents remain full
Citizens while their Civic Shares are unavailable. Launch, Homecoming, Project
commitment, injury, treatment, maturity, and death update location,
availability, life stage, or living status once, and that change remains true
in DWELL, EMBODY, TRAVEL, RISK, MEET, the Roster, and Campaign Memory.
Appendices E and H provide the exact Body-Unit and Citizen-state reference.

## 4.2 The Three Core Species

Mouse, Rabbit, and Squirrel are complete playable civilizations built upon the
same Citizen, Role, relationship, Item, consequence, and memory systems. Their
bodies change the spatial logic of Home and the characteristic advantage and
decision of Away without changing civic worth.

The illustrative maxim is: **Mouse Builds a Manor House, Rabbit Builds a
Cul-De-Sac, Squirrel Builds a Web.** These are images of civilizational shape;
Residence and Practice retain their shared functional names.

### Mouse — JOIN

Mouse lives at a quick bodily and mental tempo. Its rapid heartbeat and speed
of thought move attention among scent, sound, touch, nearby motion, and company
within a world immense at Mouse scale. Walls, roots, pipes, litter, and the
bases of human structures become edges to follow and sheltered spaces to
inhabit.

Mouse **JOINs** one protected interior directly to another. Adjoining rooms,
shared boundaries, and accumulated shelter create a Manor House of many small
spaces and many particular lives; adjacency itself supplies continuity without
requiring a passage between every pair of spaces.

### Rabbit — GATHER

Rabbit lives between vigilant stillness and explosive movement. Attention
reaches across sound, open ground, nearby companions, protective edges, and
the nearest refuge. Open space becomes useful when cover bounds it and a few
decisive leaps can reach safety.

Rabbit **GATHERs** around protected common space. Places face or preserve an
open center with mutual visibility and nearby refuge, forming one lived court
before another neighborhood buds from it.

### Squirrel — CONNECT

Squirrel lives through grasp, balance, reach, and continuous judgment in
motion. Trunks, branches, posts, signs, cables, fallen wood, and human
structures become footholds, anchors, lookouts, routes, and gaps.

Squirrel **CONNECTs** those points into a resilient Web. A safe Place belongs
to a network with credible onward movement and another way home when one link
fails; height without connection remains exposed isolation.

### Species expression at a glance

| Species | Body and civic scale | Home placement | Characteristic Away advantage | Characteristic decision |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Mouse** | 0.5 Body Unit and Civic Share per Citizen; 6 secured Carry each | JOIN adjoining protected interior | 12 secured Carry and two personal Item sets per Body Unit; a full party may contain six named Mice | which particular lives and relationships to place at risk |
| **Rabbit** | 1 Body Unit and Civic Share per Citizen | GATHER around protected common space | each Rabbit Body Unit adds an equal share of extra TRAVEL progress, reaching double progress with three Rabbit Body Units | how to use the larger practical itinerary |
| **Squirrel** | 1 Body Unit and Civic Share per Citizen | CONNECT a redundant network of anchors and routes | each Squirrel Body Unit adds 10 optional Strained Carry subject to visible Jostle risk | whether a larger possible return justifies a precarious haul |

The same bodies also change Crossing. Rabbit looks for the temporal opening and
runs one decisive line; Mouse joins three to six pavement-scale waypoints into
one continuous scurry; Squirrel commits to a flowing trajectory through moving
traffic with limited redirects. Mixed parties receive only the traits supplied
by the bodies actually present. Species advantages alter campaign flavor
without adding actions, experienced time, general aptitude, or a hierarchy of
species power. Appendices G and H hold the exact TRAVEL, Carry, Crossing,
placement, and mixed-party reference.

## 4.3 Guest Citizens

MEDIAN's world contains many more animal lives than the Colony's Core Species.
A **Guest Citizen** is a named non-Core animal welcomed through relationship
and accommodation. Kehaar in Richard Adams's *Watership Down* is a central
inspiration: a conspicuously different outsider whose practical importance
opens the way to friendship while difference remains fully visible.

Welcome grants full civic rank, a Residence, one v0.5 Body Unit, one Civic
Share, relationships, vulnerability, memory, and access to EMBODY. Each species
also carries one bounded **Signature** grounded in its body, senses, behavior,
or ecology. The Signature makes difference useful without becoming the whole
of that Citizen's identity.

### Guests at a glance

The canonical short name governs headings, tables, labels, and rules; natural
prose may use the established longer form. Expedition Guests may Launch and
receive an individually authored Home Role. Resident Guests remain at Home and
sustain the fixed Role shown below.

| Expedition Guest | Carry | Away Signature | Residence Fit |
|---|---:|---|---|
| **Raccoon** | 12 | **HANDLE — Latchwork** | dry exterior access |
| **Crow** | 9 | **HANDLE — Trialwork** | high open perch |
| **Gull** | 9 | **READ — Long View** | broad open landing |
| **Fox** | 12 | **HANDLE — Carcass Claim** | drained boundary ground |
| **Weasel** | 10 | **INTERCEDE — Drive Off** | narrow multi-exit bank |
| **Hedgehog** | 8 | **INTERCEDE — Living Cover** | concealed hedge |
| **Snake** | 6 | **SPEAK — Display** | sun-warmed shelter |
| **Mink** | 10 | **REACH — Water Reach** | safe waterline |

| Resident Guest | Role | Home Signature | Residence Fit |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Owl** | Watchkeeper | **Night Sky-watch** | high listening hollow |
| **Sparrow** | Leader | **SPEAK — Day Call** | concealed social perch |
| **Toad** | Caretaker | **Wet-Ground Care** | wet edge with dry refuge |
| **Fireflies** | Leader | **SPEAK — Lantern Procession** | dark damp flight space |
| **Groundhog** | Gardener | **Seasonal Telegraph** | deep drained ground |
| **Turtle** | Caretaker | **Water Garden Residence** | shallow water and basking |
| **Bumblebees** | Gardener | **Garden Cohabitation** | sheltered flower access |
| **Mole** | Builder | **Subsurface Diagnosis** | visible undisturbed soil |
| **Bat** | Builder | **Workshop Roost** | high dry-dark roost |
| **Pigeon** | Leader | **Gathering Loft** | open built ledge |
| **Skunk** | Watchkeeper | **SPEAK — Boundary Deterrence** | downwind boundary |
| **Possum** | Caretaker | **Hearth Annex** | ventilated receiving edge |

### Hospitality and Guest life

Hospitality begins through MEET with a named life rather than a recruitment
roll. Recognition establishes a relationship, obligation, promise, shared
interest, or unresolved tension. **Terms of Hospitality** then identify the
Residence Fit and any particular relational or safety promise required for
arrival. A Builder completes or adapts the Residence through an ordinary
Project; when the Terms are true, Arrival adds the Guest to Home and the
Roster. Acceptance, delay, refusal, and an unrealized welcome remain meaningful
campaign outcomes.

One Guest Residence ordinarily occupies one dedicated Place and houses one
Citizen. A Well-Placed Residence satisfies its single species-specific Fit as
a whole. Turtle, Bumblebees, Bat, Pigeon, and Possum instead have explicit
Signatures that incorporate their Residence into one named Practice without
creating a general mixed-use rule. Later loss of fit creates a visible
accommodation problem and may suspend a directly dependent Signature; it never
evicts the Citizen.

At Home, Guests sustain Roles, keep routines, form relationships, and appear
through movement, sound, rest, food, conversation, season, and characteristic
activity. Quiet Equilibrium can open EMBODY as a bounded species activity or a
period of Presence. Injury, absence, aging, departure, and death remain
particular and visible, while their Residences and relationships preserve what
changed.

Fireflies and Bumblebees are the two collective-bodied Citizen households in
v0.5. Each visible plurality forms one named Citizen, Body Unit, Civic Share,
Residence, Role, Signature, and continuous subject of consequence. Individual
bodies receive no separate administration; the household remains legible
through recurring formation, circulation, sound, light, and collective change.

### Expedition Guest profiles

#### **Raccoon**

Raccoon seeks dry exterior access and carries 12 secured slots. **HANDLE —
Latchwork** lets this dexterous object specialist manipulate one accessible
human-made closure or simple mechanism without revealing its contents or
guaranteeing value.

#### **Crow**

Crow occupies a high open perch, carries 9, and uses beak, feet, memory, and
experimental curiosity. **HANDLE — Trialwork** recruits one available object
for one plausible test or indirect manipulation without manufacturing a Tool
or guaranteeing the result.

#### **Gull**

Gull requires a broad open landing and carries 9 while remaining subject to
party time, Exposure, and consequence. **READ — Long View** reveals situated
context about broad geography, weather, water, traffic, or distant activity
from an eligible vantage before commitment.

#### **Fox**

Red Fox lives at a drained Colony boundary and carries 12 despite remaining
one Body Unit. **HANDLE — Carcass Claim** converts suitable matter at an
eligible carcass Node into its specified Supply as the biological counterpart
to the Render Tool effect.

#### **Weasel**

Weasel favors a narrow bank with several exits and carries 10. **INTERCEDE —
Drive Off** presses one small predator or aggressive animal away from one
immediate vulnerable stake.

#### **Hedgehog**

Hedgehog inhabits a concealed hedge and carries 8. **INTERCEDE — Living Cover**
places their defended body between danger and one injured or very small
Citizen, fragile Cargo stake, or bounded withdrawal.

#### **Snake**

Garter Snake rests in sun-warmed shelter and is the 6-Carry expedition outlier.
**SPEAK — Display** uses recognized posture, movement, and stillness to open a
fitting social response without compelling agreement.

#### **Mink**

American Mink lives at a safe waterline and carries 10. **REACH — Water Reach**
allows entry into suitable water, flooded culverts, drainage channels, or
waterlogged spaces on the party's behalf while leaving current, cold,
contamination, and consequence intact.

### Resident Guest profiles

#### **Owl**

Barred Owl inhabits a high listening hollow and sustains Watchkeeper.
**Night Sky-watch** sharpens one existing nocturnal Telegraph while Owl is
available and a Watchpost is usable.

#### **Sparrow**

Song Sparrow lives at a concealed social perch and sustains Leader. **SPEAK —
Day Call** turns one familiar daylight warning or instruction into shared
understanding during an eligible Home MEET.

#### **Toad**

American Toad moves between a wet edge and dry refuge while sustaining
Caretaker. **Wet-Ground Care** opens a Caretaker response in an eligible Garden
MEET concerning runoff, dampness, or invertebrate pressure.

#### **Fireflies**

The Firefly Family occupies dark damp flight space and sustains Leader as one
collective-bodied Citizen. **SPEAK — Lantern Procession** uses coordinated
night light to make participants easier to understand and open an eligible
win-win response.

#### **Groundhog**

Groundhog inhabits deep drained ground and sustains Gardener. **Seasonal
Telegraph** clarifies the kind of seasonal or environmental change becoming
likely early enough for civic response without predicting exact timing or
severity.

#### **Turtle**

Painted Turtle sustains Caretaker from shallow water and a safe basking place
within a Garden. **Water Garden Residence** incorporates that Residence through
an ordinary Builder Project without consuming another Place or adding yield.

#### **Bumblebees**

The Bumblebee Household sustains Gardener as one collective-bodied Citizen
within a suitable Garden. **Garden Cohabitation** saves a Place and adds 0.5
Perishable Sustenance while the host is usable and the household is available
and sustaining Gardener responsibility.

#### **Mole**

Eastern Mole inhabits visible undisturbed soil and sustains Builder.
**Subsurface Diagnosis** identifies the relevant hidden physical cause beneath
one Place or proposed Place when a Home MEET makes that ground matter.

#### **Bat**

Little Brown Bat sustains Builder from a high, dry-dark, smoke-free Workshop
roost. **Workshop Roost** incorporates the Residence through an ordinary
Builder Project without another Place, Practice Strength, output, or capacity.

#### **Pigeon**

Rock Pigeon sustains Leader from an open built ledge within a Gathering Place.
**Gathering Loft** incorporates the Residence through an ordinary Builder
Project without another Place, Practice Strength, or capacity.

#### **Skunk**

Striped Skunk sustains Watchkeeper from a downwind boundary Residence.
**SPEAK — Boundary Deterrence** projects credible toughness and opens a
favorable nonviolent response during an eligible threatening-animal Home MEET.

#### **Possum**

Possum sustains Caretaker from a ventilated outer annex of a Hearth. **Hearth
Annex** incorporates the Residence through an ordinary Builder Project without
another Place or any Caretaker Provisioning, Practice Strength, or capacity
beyond Possum's ordinary Civic Share.

Guest recruitment remains optional at every Tier. No victory condition or
essential system requires a Guest, a particular species, or a complete roster.
Appendix H provides the complete Hospitality, Residence, collective-body,
Signature, limitation, EMBODY, naming, and species-profile reference.

## 4.4 Relationships and Family

Citizens become particular friends, relatives, companions, rivals, teachers,
students, caregivers, and mourners. Relationships are qualitative truths with
their own histories, able to coexist and change without forming one affection
ladder.

The player creates opportunities for contact, keeps promises, offers care,
chooses responses, and places Citizens in consequential circumstances.
Citizens retain ownership of affection, trust, forgiveness, estrangement, and
other social responses. Meaningful formation, reversal, separation, or
reconciliation becomes visible through ordinary life, MEET, EMBODY, departure,
and return.

**Family** is the set of persistent family relationships among particular
Citizens. Parenthood, siblinghood, adoption, partnership, chosen kinship,
sustained care, and authored recognition may form overlapping families across
species, generations, Residences, and Guest or Core status. Cohabitation can
express family without defining it, and Hearth remains exclusively a Practice.

Relationships influence dialogue, Focus, advice, aid, hesitation, protection,
reunion, and consequence only when the current situation makes them relevant.
Any mechanical effect belongs to that event or owning system. Death ends a
Citizen's present action while their relationships, shared history, surviving
Tales, inheritance, grief, and memorial continuity endure. Appendix H carries
the complete relationship and family reference.

## 4.5 The Life Course

Every Citizen has an origin, a present life stage, and a possible future.
Founders begin with shared history; Wanderers and Guests bring Prior-life Tales;
Nesting, adoption, and authored arrival establish new named lives and immediate
relationships. Origin changes what accompanies a Citizen into Colony life,
never the standing of that life.

v0.5 uses two mechanically distinct stages: **Young** and **Adult**. A Young
Citizen is named, housed, related, remembered, and exposed to age-appropriate
Home life while adding ordinary needs and contributing no Civic Share. Rabbit
and Squirrel young each constitute one Body Unit; the usual pair of Mouse
littermates constitutes one Body Unit while remaining two Citizens.

**Maturity** is the visible transition into adult Civic Share and ordinary Role
eligibility after sufficient seasons and authored life events. It is a moment
recognized by family and Colony rather than an experience bar or training
rank. Adult age continues through appearance, movement, routine, relationships,
responsibility, memory, changing needs, and occasional authored changes to
availability; v0.5 has no universal Elder stage or lifespan countdown.

Death remains possible, rare, explicit, and visible. It removes present civic
contribution without deleting historical belonging: the Tale closes, the
Record remains, and relationships, Keepsakes, possessions, Places, routines,
grief, inheritance, and remembrance carry the particular absence forward.
Appendix H provides the complete origins, Young, Maturity, aging, and continuity
reference.

## 4.6 Personal Items and Focus

Personal Items help the player know who a Citizen is and remember what has
happened to them. They remain attached to particular lives while MEET choices
and resolution remain collective.

| Position | Eligible Citizens | Persistence | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Keepsake** | Every Citizen | Personally persistent | Carries emotional meaning and one bounded contextual expression. |
| **Tool** | Launch-eligible Citizens | Persistent; freely reassigned at Home | Supplies one reusable working capability Away. |
| **Supply** | Launch-eligible Citizens | Assigned for one Expedition; consumed when committed | Supplies one prepared intervention Away. |

Young Citizens and Resident Guests remain complete without inert Away slots.
Tools, Supplies, and Keepsakes occupy personal positions rather than Cargo;
body compatibility changes their physical form rather than creating a species
or Role proficiency system.

### Tools

A **Tool** is a persistent named working object. The five ordinary classes are
**Carry**, **Reach**, **Cut**, **Brace**, and **Render**. A relevant Tool appears
after the player chooses a MEET response; selecting it supplies one narrow
reusable capability, places its holder in Focus, and exposes it to that Round's
situated hazard. Multiple Tools create redundancy and possible protagonists
rather than a stacking bonus. Damage can suspend a Tool until ordinary Crafter
repair, while Fine Work can open specifically authored **Specialist Tools**. A
Citizen may discard a held Tool whenever the player chooses, permanently
freeing that Tool position unless an explicit recipient or transaction takes
the object.

### Supplies

A **Supply** is one anonymous body-scaled object prepared for one intervention
and assigned to one Launch-eligible Citizen for an Expedition.

| Supply | Direction | Prepared by |
|---|---|---|
| **Binding** | Connect or secure with one cord, strip, wrap, or fastening. | Crafter |
| **Device** | Produce one temporary physical or sensory effect through a small contrivance. | Crafter |
| **Offering** | Place one morsel or meaningful token into an encounter. | Crafter |
| **Remedy** | Treat, protect, or stabilize a body with one dose, dressing, or poultice. | Caretaker |

The authored MEET states the exact available effect. Commitment consumes the
Supply whether or not the chosen response achieves its intended outcome. A
Remedy may stabilize a Wound through Homecoming, lessen one defined immediate
effect, contain the acute Wound accompanying Maiming, or express PARLEY through
care; it cannot relieve Tharn. Unspent Supplies return to the Colony pool at
Homecoming and their positions clear. At a usable Outpost, any Citizen with an
empty Supply position may choose one available prepared Supply of any class
from Colony Stores. The selected unit leaves Stores and occupies that position;
an occupied position cannot be exchanged, and capacity remains one.

### Keepsakes

A **Keepsake** is a persistent emotional object rooted in one Citizen's life;
an empty position remains a complete life. One narrow personal circumstance may
make it relevant after the player chooses a MEET response. Selecting it places
the holder in Focus and may soften a personal consequence, preserve a
meaningful stake, or permit a contextual response. Gifting, loss, sacrifice,
succession, replacement, and memorial display occur only through an explicit
meaning-making event.

### Focus

**Focus** identifies the Citizen whom a MEET Round naturally follows without
changing the party's collective decision.

1. The MEET presents its valid choices.
2. The player chooses an Approach.
3. Relevant Tools, Supplies, Keepsakes, relationships, Signatures, and authored
   circumstances become visible.
4. The player may choose no personal-Item expression or one Tool, Supply, or
   Keepsake expression; an applicable Signature may coexist with it.
5. The chosen expression or circumstance establishes the eligible Focus set;
   one qualifying Citizen receives Focus, chosen randomly when several qualify.
